- **jcamp-dx-data-type-block-classification**: Use when you need to automatically determine the spectra type (NMR, IR, MS, UV/VIS, XRD, CV, LC/MS) of an uploaded JCAMP-DX file before routing it to the correct conversion or analysis path in the chem-spectra-app backend. - **jcamp-file-conversion**: Use when input spectra are in mzML, proprietary RAW/FID, Bruker zip, or other non-JCAMP-DX formats and must be normalized to open JCAMP-DX (IR, MS, 1H/13C NMR) for ELN storage, editing, or image generation. - **jcamp-file-parsing**: Use when you receive uploaded spectral data in JCAMP format (jcamp) as input to the /api/v1/chemspectra/file/convert endpoint, or when you need to extract and validate metadata and peak information from an existing JCAMP file before converting to another format or performing spectral analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/jcamp-file-parsing))
- **jcamp-peak-table-composition**: Use when detected peak positions must be converted into an annotated peak table/label file (e.g. IR wavenumbers with bracketed vw/w/m/s/vs intensity labels) for export as JCAMP-DX metadata or ELN transfer. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jcamp-peak-table-composition))
- **jcamp-spectrum-parsing**: Use when the input is a JCAMP-DX spectrum (or a file already converted to JCAMP-DX, e.g. mzML converted via pymzML) and the x/y data points must be extracted as the basis for peak picking, annotation, or peaks output. - **jcf-data-analysis**: Use when building and running the empirical analysis for a Journal of Corporate Finance (JCF) paper — assembling WRDS-era firm panels (Compustat/CRSP/SDC/DealScan), constructing variables, estimating with fixed effects and clustered errors, and layering robustness. - **jcf-rebuttal**: Use when preparing a revise-and-resubmit response for the Journal of Corporate Finance (JCF) — a point-by-point reply to two-or-more single-anonymized reviewers and the handling editor, reconciling divergent demands without over-claiming. - **jcrd-related-work**: 在为《计算机研究与发展》(Journal of Computer Research and Development, JCRD) 稿件撰写相关工作或文献综述时调用。覆盖综述型稿件的分类框架构建、原创稿件相关工作的 delta 定位、系统性文献检索与纳入排除标准、中英文文献并重的覆盖度、双盲评审下自引的第三人称处理、以及面向本刊专题(综述与原创并重)的综述组织。适用于把一份 JCRD 稿件的文献部分从罗列改写成有分类框架与清晰定位、既覆盖国内外进展又突出本文贡献差异的场景。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-Computer-Research-and-Development-Skills/skills/jcrd-related-work) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jcrd-reproducibility**: 在为《计算机研究与发展》(Journal of Computer Research and Development, JCRD) 稿件构建可复现性与实验可重复证据时调用。覆盖环境与依赖版本固定、随机种子与非确定性控制、数据集版本与抽取日期钉死、大模型/API 输出缓存、硬件与度量口径记录、双盲下的匿名可运行材料、诚实的可用性说明，以及让外审专家在不联系作者情况下重跑主结果的组织方式。适用于把一份 JCRD 中文稿件的实验做到可被第三方在受控条件下重复、并经得起外审可复现质疑的场景。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-Computer-Research-and-Development-Skills/skills/jcrd-reproducibility) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
